Status describes TF annotations; unmarked letters have not been independently verified as secure ink.
Totals describe supplied sign slots, not how much of an entire biblical verse survives.
Lexeme vowels and grammar are analyses; reconstructed letters are editorial readings.
Read README.txt and DSS_SOURCE_USAGE.txt before interpretation.
